# TDS/PAC original-to-integrated source reconciliation v1.0

Status: exhaustive textual crosswalk and conservative interpretation; **semantic adjudication of non-identical clauses remains open**. Prepared 2026-11-27 against PR #238 original head `cd677b3c72f9696f8be2065deb90195aefeb92e7`, immutable blueprint blob `24f3cec749362307f1d77d676ab237e2b2dd036d`, source baseline `fd993fb2d66b93fbe81ce99c5bfb50c9ec025542`. See [consistency reconciliation](DESIGN_CONSISTENCY_RECONCILIATION_v1.0.md). No change of scope, permission or acceptance status is made here.

The exact original review sources are Blueprint v1.3 Part IV Annex A (TDS) and B (PAC). Part V and the pinned repository contain the distinct integrated editions including later clarifications. Both editions are preserved; shorter wording is not evidence of deleted obligations or equivalence. This register identifies every nonblank original and integrated source line (paragraph, list item, table row or heading), with inclusive counterpart section ranges; acceptance IDs are paired exactly. This is a textual coverage inventory, not a sentence-by-sentence semantic proof. An exact textual match does not prove equivalent surrounding conditions. Candidate matches are lexical navigation aids only. No unmatched clause is deemed superseded merely because it differs. The original source line is the review unit, not an independently numbered functional requirement. Blank lines are included in the section range crosswalk but omitted from line tables.

## Dispositions and recommended authority

- SAME: identical nonblank line within mapped section, context still controls.
- AC: matching acceptance ID, compare both full scenario and evidence; original more explicit compatible checks remain applicable, no automatic equivalence or pass.
- REVIEW: nonidentical clause, candidate location where found; preserve both and adjudicate before affected implementation. Explicitly retain all compatible approved requirements, distinguish examples/targets/proposed routes from mandatory scope, and escalate genuine conflicts. Until approved resolution, accepted ADR and existing runtime contract govern current behavior; do not infer permission changes. An unmatched proposed detail remains a proposal, not a new binding SLO.
- Historical references, source status and integration instructions describe their own snapshot; later approved decisions control, not date order alone.
- Proposed owner decision OD-02: confirm cumulative compatible approved TDS/PAC obligations (including more detailed original acceptance wording), with only specific documented approved supersession; unresolved semantic cases require M0 review and approval when material. This is not a claim that all paraphrases have been adjudicated, nor authorization to change ADR 0015.

| TP-01 | PAC original §2.3 / integrated §2 critical reconciliation; original AC-03/04 and integrated AC-03/04 | Same intended future security outcome; conflict with current ADR 0015 | OD-01; audit current effective access; successor ADR and populated upgrade before changing managed grants. |
| TP-02 | PAC original L431–437 / integrated L232 | Additional explicit provisional measurement inputs, not achieved or binding SLOs | Preserve 100 org/10,000 staff first-50 directory p95 ≤1 s; bounded overview p95 ≤2 s; health 30 s refresh/90 s stale; support 60 min default, emergency 30 min max candidate, step-up 5 min; daily verified backup, candidate RPO 24 h/RTO 4 h. Validate at M0, document any adjustment, PAC-AC-37 and AC-25–27. |
| TP-03 | TDS original L277–284 / integrated L194 | Corresponding provisional targets with condensed operational wording | Retain exact original limits and conditions (not final SLO); partition larger jobs with explicit review; M0 tests, AC-21/22. |
| TP-04 | TDS original AC-03–07 / integrated AC-03–07 | Same IDs, additional explicit evidence wording (headings, save, sample vs actual print dimensions, purpose checks) | Test both editions including original details; no admission dependency for synthetic preview. |
| TP-05 | TDS original AC-24 / integrated AC-24 and later AGIS/CID clarifications | Historical card reissue wording is ambiguous about credential replacement | Explicit refresh/re-render/reprint vs replace credential; preserve old artifact and current eligibility. AGIS/CID govern actual credential commands. |
| TP-06 | PAC original §3.3 and §22 / integrated §3 and §22 | Proposed single-owner governance and separate transfer/recovery decisions, not a new account flag | OD-01 and owner governance M0; never promote first registration or infer data authority. |
| TP-07 | Both original and integrated PAC §21; TDS §13 | Matching acceptance IDs; paraphrased clauses/evidence are not byte-identical | Preserve all 40 PAC and 25 TDS scenarios and original detailed conditions; acceptance evidence remains not run. |
